Replay works past the old 50-run scan window; the same key with a different payload conflicts with `WORKFLOW_IDEMPOTENCY_CONFLICT`; the legacy scan is retained only as a fallback for pre-v4 databases.
- **PostgreSQL parity is now VERIFIED, not BLOCKED_EXTERNAL:** `postgres_store.py` implements the real store on PostgreSQL 14 (identity columns, `?`→`%s`, `INSERT OR IGNORE`→`ON CONFLICT DO NOTHING`, deferrable FK for the identity migration, PL/pgSQL immutability triggers, savepoint-aware transactions, `pg_advisory_xact_lock` write locks). 17 integration tests run in isolated per-test schemas: migration repeatability, card/translation revisions, stale-write rejection, insert-only semantics, FK and unique constraints, Unicode/JSON/timezone fidelity, database-level audit immutability, cursor history, tenant/project isolation, two-thread races, rollback, and the v2→v3 corpus migration.
- **Defects found and fixed:** SQLite serialised concurrent writers through `BEGIN IMMEDIATE` while PostgreSQL did not, so two concurrent writers could both succeed — fixed with `_lock_card`/`_lock_translation` hooks; an approval token could be spent by a different administrator — now bound to approved actor, run id and review hash; two unkeyed `start()` calls in the same second collided — unkeyed seeds now carry a nonce; `tools/knowledge_inventory.py` reported 0 validated for 1030 validated cards — fixed with a regression test and a hash check; partial ingestion could be recorded ambiguously — pinned to `partial` with failures listed and a `partial` audit outcome.

## Database migration and repository parity — 2026-11-13 (merged; PR #1819)

- **Base and merge:** branch cut from merged `main` and squash-merged as **`a789d7f`** via PR #1819. Scope is Issue #1799 Workstream 5 for the stores changed by Workstreams 2 and 4: document metadata/audit/content, OCR, retention/disposition and exchange run/idempotency.
- **Parity audit:** SQLite and PostgreSQL catalogs were compared for every changed store; one real gap was found — SQLite did not declare `idx_exchange_run_scope` — and the index is now created additively in both dialects on fresh install and on upgrade.
- **Idempotent content insert:** content-version insert uses `INSERT ... ON CONFLICT DO NOTHING` and resolves the race through the idempotency index. A same-key replay returns the stored version; a different key racing for the same version fails closed as `DOCUMENT_CONTENT_VERSION_CONFLICT` instead of surfacing a driver error.
- **Repeatable initialization:** fresh install, double `initialize()`, legacy pre-scan JSON upgrade, preserved rows and index upgrade are covered on SQLite and live PostgreSQL. Migrations remain additive (`CREATE TABLE/INDEX IF NOT EXISTS`); nothing is dropped or rebuilt.
- **Concurrency and fidelity:** live two-thread races with the same key and with different keys, plus timezone, microsecond and JSON round-trip fidelity, run against PostgreSQL rather than being inferred from SQLite.
- **Contract:** `docs/engineering/P6_DATABASE_MIGRATION_PARITY_CONTRACT.md` records the additive rules, parity matrix and the explicit limitation that report and schedule stores remain SQLite-only.
- **Verification:** full SQLite suite → **3859 passed, 150 skipped**; live PostgreSQL with `CONSTRUCTION_PM_POSTGRES_DSN` → **4035 passed, 20 skipped**; `tests/integration` run twice consecutively against the same database → **405 passed** both times.
- **Not claimed:** no destructive migration, no report/schedule PostgreSQL store, no production database change or deployment.

## XML/XLSX import security hardening — 2026-11-13 (merged; PR #1823)

- **Base and merge:** squash-merged as **`25096d0`** via PR #1823. Scope is Issue #1799 Workstream 6 boundary hardening for Primavera/MS Project XML and XLSX exchange import.
- **XML:** both XML codecs reject any DTD/entity declaration (`XML_DECLARATION_REJECTED`) and payloads above 10 MB (`XML_PAYLOAD_TOO_LARGE`) before parsing, closing XXE and entity-expansion exposure.
- **XLSX archive:** the zip container is inspected before openpyxl. Macro projects are rejected (`EXCHANGE_XLSX_MACROS_REJECTED`), total uncompressed size is capped at 50 MB (`EXCHANGE_XLSX_TOO_LARGE`), loading is read-only and link-disabled, and rows are capped at 50,000 (`EXCHANGE_XLSX_TOO_MANY_ROWS`).
- **Negative tests:** XXE, billion-laughs entity declarations, XML/XLSX size bounds, macro injection, `Content-Disposition` header injection and filename normalization.
- **Audit record:** `docs/engineering/P6_BACKEND_SECURITY_AUDIT.md` lists reviewed controls, evidence and residual risk, and explicitly does not claim an independent security review; the review quota failure was not converted into a fabricated result.
- **Verification:** full SQLite suite → **3891 passed, 150 skipped**; live PostgreSQL → **4067 passed, 20 skipped**.
- **Not claimed:** no independent third-party security review, no WAF/network controls, no production deployment or secret change.

## Production readiness, health, metrics and restore consistency — 2026-11-13 (merged; PR #1824)

- **Base and merge:** branch cut from `25096d0`, rebased onto `b233a94` before final exact-head CI, squash-merged as **`fe564ca`** via PR #1824. Scope is Issue #1799 Workstream 7.
- **Configuration validation:** `validate_environment` checks environment, database, object storage, scanner, OCR, job queue, retention and observability. Production rejects SQLite, local disk, the reference scanner, in-process queue, disabled retention and non-JSON logs. Reports carry stable codes and variable names only — never DSNs, bucket names, keys or values.
- **Readiness:** `GET /api/health/live` returns 200 while serving; `GET /api/health/ready` returns 200 for `ready`/`degraded` and 503 for `not_ready`; other methods return 405 and HEAD returns no body. Mandatory and optional (OCR) components are distinguished, and configuration is never presented as a live probe — a required component without a real probe reports `PROBE_NOT_CONFIGURED`.
- **Correlation, logs and metrics:** every response carries a validated or generated `X-Request-ID`; the middleware sets a correlation contextvar, logs method, path without query, status and duration, and records bounded `http_requests_total` / `http_request_duration_ms` using route templates rather than raw paths. `OperationalMetrics` validates names and labels, caps series and samples, and is thread-safe. Uploads, OCR jobs, scheduled report runs and storage reconciliation emit counters and structured events without content, secrets or ids as labels.
- **Staging smoke:** `run_staging_smoke` and `scripts/staging_smoke.py` issue GET requests only, accept `degraded` where required components are healthy, and return distinct exit codes for pass, probe failure and invalid target.
- **Deployment template:** `infra/deploy/production.env.example`, `deployment.template.yaml`, README and `P6_PRODUCTION_READINESS_CONTRACT.md` document mandatory variables, probes and the smoke command. The manifest is a template with `REPLACE_*` values and was **not applied**.
- **Backup/restore consistency:** `verify_document_versions` / `verify_document_scope` compare metadata against stored bytes and SHA-256, detect missing, metadata-only and tampered restores, and omit payloads and storage refs from reports. Verification never repairs, deletes or mutates records. Interrupted restore and repeated verification are now covered explicitly.
- **Verification at final head `30f4f52`:** full SQLite suite → **3938 passed, 150 skipped**; live PostgreSQL → **4114 passed, 20 skipped**; PR checks 3.11/3.12/3.13, PostgreSQL, and four typecheck jobs all **success**. Merged `main` runs for PostgreSQL Sync State Integration and Client Typecheck succeeded; ConstructionPM CI for `fe564ca` was observed green via the merge gate.
- **External gates:** authorized staging deployment and smoke execution, real object storage/scanner/OCR services and credentials, and the licensed V26 parity dataset remain external. No production infrastructure, secret, DNS, billing or customer data was touched and no production deployment is claimed.

## Operational scanner adapter and scan timestamps — 2026-11-13 (merged; PR #1843)

- **Base and merge:** branch cut from merged `main` `fe564ca` and squash-merged as **`b80dd8f`** via PR #1843. Scope is the remaining Issue #1799 Workstream 4 scanner-boundary gap.
- **Adapter:** `HttpDocumentScanner` posts raw bytes to one configured endpoint, forwards the declared content type, optionally sends a bearer token, refuses redirects, caps request/response size and requires a complete `status`/`engine`/`engine_version` JSON verdict. Any transport, timeout, non-200, invalid or oversized response fails closed as `DocumentScanUnavailable`; `error`/`fatal`/`timeout`/`unavailable` map to `failed`. Exception text contains only a code or HTTP status — never the endpoint token or payload.
- **Selection:** `scanner_from_environment()` selects `reference` or `external` from `CONSTRUCTION_PM_SCANNER`, requires an HTTPS endpoint for `external`, and never falls back to an implicit engine. The deployment template documents the optional token and timeout variables.
- **Timestamp:** `DocumentContentVersion.scan_scanned_at` records the timezone-aware scan time; legacy rows deserialize with `null` and no migration is required. A naive clock is rejected.
- **Tests:** 9 adapter tests (clean/infected/failed, header and body forwarding, 5xx, invalid/incomplete/oversized response, redirect refusal, unreachable service, concurrent scans, endpoint/token/timeout/payload validation, token non-leakage, factory selection), 3 upload-timestamp tests, and live PostgreSQL timestamp round-trip plus legacy `null`.
- **Verification at head `1918a1e`:** full SQLite suite → **3980 passed, 168 skipped**; live PostgreSQL → **4174 passed, 20 skipped**; all 7 PR checks **success**.
- **External gate:** a deployed scanner service, its credentials/licence and quarantine review workflow remain external. The adapter is tested against a local HTTP service only; no production scanner verification is claimed.

## Full-corpus PostgreSQL verification and multilingual completion — 2026-11-13 (local; publication blocked)

- **Base and branch:** cut from `main` `f07364f02eb3dc9fa6bfc5301eb523d3edab7e52` (after PR #1845) on `feat/knowledge-corpus-postgres-verification-2026-11-13`. Implementation and evidence commit: **`a2a809f8af7a0c5784ce0d7fce4ae0dcdf37eb13`**. No prior branch, commit or file was reverted, rewritten, duplicated or force-pushed.
- **Publication: BLOCKED_EXTERNAL.** The temporary GitHub credential used for PR #1839/#1842 was deliberately deleted; `GIT_ASKPASS`, token environment variables and stored credentials are absent. The branch and commit are complete and green locally, but no PR exists for this slice. Do not treat this ledger entry as a merged result.
- **PostgreSQL write-loss defect (found and fixed):** an owned `PostgresKnowledgeStore` connection used `autocommit=False`, so a plain read opened an implicit transaction, every later `transaction()` became a savepoint, and closing the connection rolled back writes the caller had already observed. The owned connection now uses `autocommit=True`; `_transaction()` issues real BEGIN/COMMIT. Regression: `test_postgres_store.py::test_owned_connection_persists_writes_made_after_a_read`.
- **Full-corpus run:** PostgreSQL 14.24, schema v4, database `kb_corpus` — **1090 cards, 1539 topics, 0 translations, 0 events, 465 audit rows**; SQLite comparison from the same sources — **1090 cards, 1539 topics, 0 translations, 0 events, 453 audit rows**. 982 HTML Help + 557 User Guide topics, all `converted`, 449 `topic_merged` decisions. Integrity checks: no duplicate ids, topic keys or card content signatures; every card has a topic; every hash is sha256; every URL is official; no orphan translations.
- **Parity/repeatability:** `tools/knowledge_corpus_verify.py` reports `ok: true`, `parity.equal: true`, `repeat_ingest.unchanged: true`; the second ingestion added no cards, topics or translations and only recorded run bookkeeping. Audit totals differ only because PostgreSQL was ingested twice during recovery testing; corpus-affecting audits are compared exactly.
- **Partial recovery:** an interrupted User Guide run converted 30/557 topics with a non-zero exit; the continuation converted the remaining 527 with 0 failures, reaching the authoritative 557 with no duplicates. Evidence: `docs/workspace/evidence/knowledge-corpus-postgres-2026-11-13/partial-run-user-guide.json` and `resume-run-user-guide.json`.
- **Inventory and tooling:** `tools/knowledge_inventory.py` now measures SQLite or PostgreSQL through one read-only contract; `tools/ingest_v26_help.py` accepts `--dsn`; `tools/knowledge_corpus_verify.py` is the new machine-readable corpus verifier. The 1030 → 1090 card shift is explained by stricter content signatures: previously merged pages that differed only in body prose are now distinct cards, and no duplicate signature remains.
- **Translation inventory:** 1030 cards × 9 target locales = 9270 slots; 9 filled (0.10%), 0 published, 0 approved, 9261 missing; every seed is `translated`/`pending`, so `answerable` is 0 in all locales. The live corpus contains 0 translations: ingestion never invents translations and never relabels English as localized text.
- **Multilingual retrieval defect fixed:** search may match localized text, but an unapproved or stale translation no longer counts as answerable. Such a hit sets `fallback_used: true`, and the assistant cites the reviewed English source title instead of the unreviewed localized title. `test_search.py::test_unreviewed_localized_translation_matches_but_is_marked_as_fallback` and `test_assistant.py::test_unapproved_translation_is_treated_as_fallback` pin the behaviour.
- **Assistant evidence states:** `AssistantAnswer.evidence_state` is one of `verified`, `unpublished`, `missing`, `insufficient`, `conflicting`; localized messages exist for every supported locale. A reader never sees an unpublished match; a reviewer is told `unpublished` without citations. Two published cards with the same title but different content produce a `conflicting` clarification instead of a single answer.

All return `kind: answer`, the correct `response_language`, `direction` (`rtl` for fa/ar), `fallback_used` true for non-English seeds, the official `docs.oracle.com` URL and the source card id. It also proves unpublished-draft exclusion, `missing` knowledge, and tenant/project isolation.
- **Agent 1 contract:** every endpoint in `CUBI_KNOWLEDGE_AGENT1_INTEGRATION_CONTRACT.md` is implemented and now exercised at the HTTP layer, including EPS, audit, memory, workflow get/back/skip/abandon and approval; the exact mapping is in `agent1-integration-checklist.md`. No UI or second assistant shell was added.

All seed rows are `translated`/`pending`, `cubi_authored`, machine=0, no reviewer, no source binding, answerable=0. Seven product seeds need human review; the Russian record needs none.

Retrieval covers the eight product languages, RTL/LTR, verified citations, fallback citation title and unpublished exclusion.

Full suite against one dedicated PostgreSQL database (`cubi_full_suite_20261113`) run twice: **4223 passed, 1 skipped** both times. ## P6 Gantt baseline windows — 2026-11-14 (branch `feat/vercel-preview-fixtures`, PR #1923)

- **Gap found:** `POST /p6/baselines/capture` stored baseline metadata and immutable activity rows, but no read exposed those planned windows. The Gantt baseline layer, status bar, Summary → Display Dates Baseline row and Layout Options toggle therefore stayed empty even after a successful capture, and the earlier ACT-011 entry correctly recorded "baseline bars remain unavailable".
- **Read boundary:** new `P6BaselineActivityAPI` (`src/construction_pm/p6_baseline_activity_api.py`) serves `GET /api/projects/{id}/p6/baselines/{baseline_id}/activities` (`p6-baseline-activity-api.v1`, `kind: p6_baseline_activity_list`) directly over the existing `P6BaselineActivityRepository` — no calculation, shift or fabricated date. It requires `PROJECT_READ`, rechecks tenant/project scope, returns 404 `BASELINE_NOT_FOUND`, 400 for invalid ids/paths and 403 `AUTHORIZATION_PERMISSION_DENIED`; wired into `preview/runtime.py` and `http/production_composition.py`. The capture `compare` route was not reused because the preview's `current_activity_service` is empty and it returns `BASELINE_UPDATE_CURRENT_EMPTY`.
- **Frontend projection:** `loadBaselineActivityDates`/`parseBaselineActivities` in `apps/web/src/workspace-cost.ts` validate the contract; `main.ts` selects the newest PRIMARY baseline (or the only one) per project scope and reloads on open; `applyBaselineDateFacts` in `apps/web/src/workspace-model.ts` writes the `activity.baseline_start`/`baseline_finish` cells before every render; `renderGanttActivityRow` draws the baseline bar in row 3, the status bar names the baseline, Summary dates shows the stored window, and the Layout Options baseline/progress-line/Data Date toggles persist through `metadata.presentation` with the saved layout.
- **Defect fixed:** `captureWorkspaceDrafts`/`restoreWorkspaceDrafts` treated the Layout Options presentation controls as user drafts, so a draft captured from an earlier render could restore a checked `Show baseline bars` over the authoritative saved presentation. Those controls are now excluded.
- **Code and evidence:** code **`1d22269f`**; `apps/web/e2e/activity-baseline-v26.mjs` (contract `cubi-activity-baseline-v26.v1`) at `1d22269f0f3a8df07e725c05775de893740e9c7a` — 13 baseline bars matching 13 API rows, row 3 inside each activity row, no bars before capture, status bar name, Summary window equality, toggle off → reload → hidden and unchecked, toggle on → 13 bars restored, 1920×1080 0 px overflow, zero exceptions/console errors/5xx. Bundle `docs/workspace/evidence/activity-baseline-v26-2026-11-14/`.
- **Tests at the same code:** `tests/test_p6_baseline_activity_api.py` 14 passed (ordering, empty baseline returns no rows, unknown/invalid id, `PROJECT_READ`, cross-scope, HTTP 200/400/403/404), `tests/test_p6_baseline_capture.py` 16 passed, web `npm test` 1106 + 14 passed, `npm run typecheck` and `npm run build` clean.
- **Remaining gaps:** variance/compare columns are unaudited (preview current-activity service empty); automatic selection picks the newest PRIMARY or the sole baseline, so multiple non-PRIMARY baselines require an explicit chooser that this slice does not add.

## WS-008 full-screen and responsive workspace, shell keyboard reachability — 2026-11-13 (branch `ws-008-responsive-workspace`)

- **Scope:** the WS-008 owner requirement (full screen plus 1280×720 / 1366×768 / 1920×1080 / landscape-phone behaviour) and the Phase 2 keyboard/accessibility requirement for the Activity workspace shell.
- **Defect found and fixed:** the shell bound Alt+mnemonic, F1 and command chords to the desktop host element, so they were dropped whenever focus sat on `document.body` — the state left by clicking a non-focusable activity row or the workspace background. `bindShellKeydown` now also registers the same handlers on `document`, skips targets already inside the host, respects an already-`defaultPrevented` chord, and lets a focused text field or an open modal dialog keep the chord. Code `cb9e931f65ca8d81616f15f84be0f0b9514f1da9` (`apps/web/src/workspace-desktop-shell.ts`).
- **Acceptance evidence:** `docs/workspace/evidence/workspace-responsive-v26-2026-11-13/`, contract `cubi-workspace-responsive-v26.v1`, suite `apps/web/e2e/workspace-responsive-v26.mjs`, 11/11 scenarios at `cb9e931f`, zero exceptions/console errors/5xx, `scheduleMutations: []`, screenshots 01–10. Measured: zero page overflow at all four viewports; Gantt 592 → 635 → 912 px at 1280/1366/1920 with 554/554 rows; 844×390 uses the `#workspace` scroller (295 px) to reach the details pane and scrolls the table 203 px internally; six menus stay inside the short viewport; real full-screen entry/exit with aria-pressed and label sync; table/Gantt splitter drag 36.74 %, keyboard ±2, clamp 25 %, reload persistence and reset; details splitter 70 → 72 → 80, drag 66, reload, reset; Persian RTL zero overflow.
- **Tests at the code SHA:** 1026 `tsx` + 14 `node --test` pass; `tsc --noEmit`, `tsc -p tsconfig.test.json --noEmit` and `npm run build` pass.
- **Gaps:** no touch splitter gesture or pinch zoom; the landscape cell relies on the workspace scroller rather than a compact touch layout; fixed-position menus below 760 px are not re-anchored on page scroll; only the 100 % zoom level is covered.
- **Process note:** the ACT-020 ledger addendum landed as a direct commit on `main` (`28e93003`) before this branch; it is documentation-only and already published. From this slice onward every change, including ledger entries, goes through a reviewed PR.

## ACT-021 activity table keyboard navigation and roving tab stop — 2026-11-13 (branch `grid-keyboard-navigation`)

- **Scope:** Phase 2 keyboard/accessibility for the Activities table; new requirement ACT-021 in `docs/workspace/architecture/v26-window-traceability-and-acceptance-matrix.md`.
- **Base and code:** branch created from main `8c1c386f22e7a5fe3f55a4c5ed03d34e8443b3e5` (WS-008 merge). Code commit **`7c4b5a5bcbcec749fd1bd9abc6a792a9ca3a53e6`** touches `apps/web/src/workspace-view.ts`, `apps/web/src/main.ts`, `apps/web/src/workspace-view.test.ts` and the new suite `apps/web/e2e/activity-grid-keyboard-v26.mjs`.
- **Defects found and fixed:** (1) every activity row carried `tabindex="0"`, so the grid had 554 Tab stops; (2) a selection re-render replaced the DOM and dropped focus to `<body>`, handing the next chord to the shell bindings; (3) `applyIncrementalWorkspaceUpdate` moved `aria-selected` but not the Tab stop. The table now has one roving Tab stop (primary row, else the first activity), the incremental path keeps it in sync, and `focusActivityRow` restores focus on the next animation frame after table/Gantt selection.
- **Behaviour:** ArrowDown/ArrowUp walk the painted order including WBS bands, Home/End jump to the ends, PageDown/PageUp move ten rows, Shift extends from the stable anchor, Ctrl/Meta moves focus only, band rows take focus without becoming the primary record, and all keys are `preventDefault`ed.
- **Acceptance evidence:** `docs/workspace/evidence/activity-grid-keyboard-v26-2026-11-13/`, contract `cubi-activity-grid-keyboard-v26.v1`, 8/8 scenarios at `7c4b5a5b`, zero exceptions/console errors/5xx, `scheduleMutations: []`, six screenshots. Measured: one Tab stop `A100`; 561 painted rows (554 activities + 7 bands) with ArrowDown 2→3 / ArrowUp 3→2; Shift range of 2 with counter 2; Ctrl+Arrow focus 4 without selection change; End 560 / Home 0 / PageDown +10 / PageUp back; collapsing `WBS-RPS-PM` 561→551 and sequence 3→4; Enter on A110 binds the details; fa RTL 2→3→2; read model still 554 with zero writes.
- **HTML Help:** [Using Shortcut Keys](https://docs.oracle.com/cd/G48902_01/client_help/en_US/63783.htm) and [Select multiple items](https://docs.oracle.com/cd/G48902_01/client_help/en_US/select_multiple_items.htm); arrow traversal and the single Tab stop are a CUBI accessibility requirement with no dedicated Oracle topic. No Oracle branding is reproduced.
- **Tests at the code SHA:** 1027 `tsx` + 14 `node --test` pass (the new roving-tab-stop case is in `workspace-view.test.ts`); `tsc --noEmit`, `tsc -p tsconfig.test.json --noEmit` and `npm run build` pass.
- **Gaps:** no screen-reader audit, no cell-level left/right traversal, no combined Shift+Home/End or Ctrl+Home/End, no type-ahead, and the roving stop follows the primary selected row rather than a remembered last-focused row when selection changes externally.

## SEC-007 legal-hold and disposition authorization — 2026-11-13

- **Finding:** `DocumentRetentionService.set_policy` and `dispose` accepted `PROJECT_WRITE`, so a planner could lift a legal hold and immediately destroy matured content. Document approval in the same aggregate already required `project_admin`.
- **Fix (code `d2aa3419`):** `set_policy` now also requires `PROJECT_ADMIN` when a hold is placed, lifted, or changed while active; `dispose` requires `PROJECT_ADMIN` unconditionally. Ordinary retention-window edits on an unheld document still accept `PROJECT_WRITE`. Denials keep the existing `DOCUMENT_PERMISSION_DENIED` code (the review’s suggested `DOCUMENT_AUTHORIZATION_REQUIRED` name was not introduced, to avoid a second code for the same boundary).
- **Tests:** `tests/test_document_retention.py::test_legal_hold_and_disposition_require_project_admin` covers planner denial for placing a hold, lifting a hold and disposing, plus a successful admin lift. The retention harness and live PostgreSQL retention test now use the `project_admin` grant. `pytest tests/test_document_retention.py tests/test_document_application_reads.py tests/test_document_http_routes.py tests/test_preview_documents.py tests/test_document_binary_storage.py` = **41 passed**.
- **Docs:** `docs/audits/BACKEND_SECURITY_REVIEW_2026-11-13.md` register and SEC-007 section updated; SEC-007 removed from the open-findings gate. No UI change.

## SEC-016 document search LIKE escaping — 2026-11-13

- **Finding:** document-list `search` was bound as `%{search}%` without `ESCAPE`, so `%` and `_` acted as wildcards and any length was accepted.
- **Fix (code `2b2873b3`):** `_escape_like` escapes backslash, `%` and `_`; the Postgres and SQLite queries add `ESCAPE '\'`; `MAX_DOCUMENT_SEARCH_LENGTH = 200` rejects longer terms with the existing `INVALID_DOCUMENT_SEARCH_FILTER` code.
- **Tests:** `tests/test_document_sqlite_store.py` adds literal matches for `50%` and `A_B`, asserts bare `%` and `_` match nothing, and rejects a 201-character search. `pytest tests/test_document_sqlite_store.py tests/test_document_http_routes.py tests/test_preview_documents.py tests/test_document_retention.py` = **31 passed**.
- **Docs:** security register and SEC-016 section updated; SEC-016 removed from the open-findings gate.

## SEC-025 reference AI permission adapter — 2026-11-13

- **Finding:** `ReferenceAIToolPermissionAdapter.allowed` returned only `proposal.tool_name in self.allowed_tools`, ignoring `subject_id` and scope; nothing in `src/` or `apps/` instantiates it.
- **Fix (code `3754a0af`):** the class is documented as test-only and returns `False` when `subject_id` is missing or blank. Covered by `test_reference_adapter_never_authorizes_a_tool_without_a_subject` (None, whitespace, and a valid subject). `pytest tests/test_ai_action_boundary.py` = **16 passed**.
- **Docs:** security register and SEC-025 section updated; SEC-025 removed from the open-findings gate. No runtime wiring changed.

## SEC-018 unexpected document/report faults now 5xx — 2026-11-13

- **Finding:** `document_application.error_dto` and `report_application.error_dto` fell through to `validation_error(type(exc).__name__, str(exc))`, so adapter/driver failures became 400 responses carrying internal class names and text.
- **Fix (code `ebaec38a`):** both fall-throughs return a fixed persistence error (`DOCUMENT_INTERNAL_ERROR`, `REPORT_INTERNAL_ERROR`) with an empty message. Known domain mappings are unchanged.
- **Tests:** the existing integration assertion now expects `APIErrorCategory.PERSISTENCE` and `DOCUMENT_INTERNAL_ERROR`; new `test_unexpected_report_fault_is_a_server_error_without_internal_text` proves the report message does not leak. `pytest tests/integration/test_document_application.py tests/test_report_api.py` = **11 passed**.
- **Boundary:** SEC-017 (per-site reflected codes across 125 `_error` call sites) stays open; this change closes only the catch-all fall-throughs.
- **Docs:** security register and SEC-018 section updated; SEC-018 removed from the open-findings gate.

## SEC-019 AI action self-approval policy — 2026-11-13

- **Finding:** `AIActionRuntime._decide_in_transaction` passed the same authenticated subject as proposer and approver, so "separate human approval" did not require a second account.
- **Fix (code `9c362d15`):** new `AIActionApprovalPolicy(allow_self_approval=True, require_distinct_approver_for=frozenset())`. `AIActionApprovalService` denies an approval when the authenticated actor equals `proposal.requested_by` and the policy requires separation, recording a denied decision and raising `AI_ACTION_SELF_APPROVAL_FORBIDDEN`. `AIActionRuntime` accepts and forwards the policy; the preview runtime composes it from `CUBI_AI_REQUIRE_DISTINCT_APPROVER` (off by default, so existing single-operator deployments are unchanged). Rejections and a distinct approver remain allowed.
- **Tests:** `test_policy_refuses_self_approval_and_audits_the_denial`, `test_policy_can_require_a_distinct_approver_for_listed_tools_only`, `test_default_policy_preserves_self_approval_for_existing_deployments`, and `test_self_approval_policy_denies_proposer_and_leaves_proposal_proposed` (status stays `proposed`, no executor call, denial audited). `pytest tests/test_ai_action_* tests/test_ai_assistant_orchestration.py tests/test_preview_runtime.py` = **89 passed**.
- **Boundary:** the policy is opt-in. Destructive-tool enforcement requires the operator to list tool names in `require_distinct_approver_for`; no built-in "destructive" classifier was invented for the two registered create tools.
- **Docs:** security register and SEC-019 section updated; SEC-019 removed from the open-findings gate.

## SEC-020 knowledge ingestion redirect refusal — 2026-11-13

- **Finding:** `knowledge/ingest.py` used `urlopen` with default redirect handling; the scheme/host allowlist was checked only on the first URL, so a redirect could pivot to another host.
- **Fix (code `c55b6535`):** `UrllibFetcher` uses an injected/default opener with a `_RejectRedirects` handler that raises instead of following; redirect status codes map to `KNOWLEDGE_FETCH_REDIRECT_REFUSED`. Ingestion remains an operator CLI surface with no HTTP route.
- **Tests:** `test_urllib_fetcher_refuses_a_redirect_without_a_second_request` (local HTTP redirect server; the target receives zero requests) and `test_urllib_fetcher_maps_a_refused_redirect_to_a_static_code`. `pytest tests/knowledge` = **255 passed, 24 skipped**.
- **Docs:** security register and SEC-020 section updated; SEC-020 removed from the open-findings gate.

## SEC-023 anonymous readiness detail redaction — 2026-11-13

- **Finding:** `/api/health/ready` is answered before authentication and returned the full readiness payload, including each component's `detail` string.
- **Fix (code `9dbe8e17`):** anonymous responses keep `status`, `generated_at`, and per-component `name`/`requirement`/`status` but omit `detail`. Full detail is returned only when a deployment injects `readiness_detail_allowed` and it approves the request; a missing or raising callback is treated as anonymous. The unconfigured-readiness 503 body no longer carries `READINESS_NOT_CONFIGURED`.
- **Tests:** four new cases in `tests/test_production_readiness.py` cover anonymous redaction, callback-approved detail, a raising callback, and the unconfigured body. `pytest tests/test_production_readiness.py tests/test_observability.py tests/test_staging_smoke.py tests/test_schedule_query_production_composition.py` = **51 passed**.
- **Boundary:** the production composition does not currently have a session store at the middleware layer, so it keeps the safe redacted default; exposing detail requires an operator-supplied callback.
- **Docs:** security register and SEC-023 section updated; SEC-023 removed from the open-findings gate.

## SEC-013 document-record references in reconciliation — 2026-11-13

- **Finding:** `_metadata_references` built the live-reference set only from content versions. An object referenced solely by `DocumentRecord.storage_ref` therefore classified as `orphan_object` and could be deleted by a non-dry run.
- **Fix (code `c921ac8b`):** `DocumentStore.list_all(tenant_id=..., project_id=...)` was added to the protocol, SQLite and PostgreSQL stores for snapshot use (the route `list` stays bounded by its own filters). `DocumentStorageReconciler` accepts an optional `document_store`, snapshots document-level refs inside the same fail-closed metadata try block, and returns `match` with detail `document_record_reference` when an object has no version row but is still held by a document record.
- **Tests:** `test_document_record_only_reference_is_never_treated_as_an_orphan` seeds a versioned object to map the scope, then a legacy record-only object; the control run reports one orphan, the fixed run reports two matches, preserves the file and writes no cleanup row. `test_document_list_all_respects_scope` covers unfiltered and tenant/project-filtered order. `pytest tests/test_document_storage_reconciliation.py tests/test_document_sqlite_store.py` = **37 passed**.
- **Boundary:** the reconciler has no HTTP route; production composition does not construct it, so callers must pass `document_store` to get the protection.
- **Docs:** security register and SEC-013 section updated; SEC-013 removed from the open-findings gate.

## SEC-012 document content-reference binding — 2026-11-13

- **Finding:** document create/update accepted `storage_ref`/`content_hash` from the request with shape checks only, and the legacy serve branch returned `local:v1/` bytes without consulting dispositions. A project writer could bind a document to arbitrary in-scope bytes that never passed sniff/scan/versioning, or retarget an approved document, and disposal could not reach the legacy binding.
- **Fix (code `afb18263`):** `create` resolves any `local:v1/` reference through the storage adapter in the caller's tenant/project scope with the declared digest and rejects failures as `DOCUMENT_CONTENT_REFERENCE_UNVERIFIED`. `update` rejects any change to the pair as `DOCUMENT_CONTENT_REFERENCE_IMMUTABLE`; only `upload_content` may change it. The legacy download branch checks `list_dispositions` first and raises `DOCUMENT_CONTENT_DISPOSED`.
- **Tests:** create rejects an unresolved legacy ref and accepts one that resolves and serves it; update cannot retarget; a legacy document with a disposition is never served. `pytest tests/test_document_*.py tests/test_preview_documents.py tests/test_preview_runtime.py tests/integration/test_document_application.py tests/integration/test_document_retention_postgres_live.py` = **189 passed, 1 skipped**.
- **Boundary:** a `local:v1/` object already stored in the same project remains bindable by a writer who can read that project; the reference is now hash-verified and served bytes are real project bytes, not fabricated or cross-scope. Metadata-only deployments without a storage adapter have no serve path.
- **Docs:** security register and SEC-012 section updated; SEC-012 removed from the open-findings gate.

## SEC-010 bounded document lists and scoped snapshots — 2026-11-13

- **Finding:** content/document `list_all` had no `LIMIT` and accepted an empty scope; reconciliation called it unscoped; `DocumentStore.list` and the list route were unbounded.
- **Fix (code `6112ddf3`):** both `list_all` implementations require a tenant (`DOCUMENT_SCOPE_REQUIRED`) and read at most `MAX_REFERENCE_SNAPSHOT_ROWS + 1`, raising `DOCUMENT_REFERENCE_SNAPSHOT_TOO_LARGE` so reconciliation aborts instead of deleting from a truncated snapshot. `DocumentStorageReconciler.reconcile` requires a tenant (`INVALID_RECONCILIATION_SCOPE`); tenant-wide sweeps must iterate tenants. `DocumentStore.list`, the service/API and the `GET /documents` route accept `limit` (default 200, max 500) with `INVALID_DOCUMENT_PAGE_LIMIT`.
- **Tests:** page bound and limit validation on a 205-document store; tenant-scope requirement for both snapshot APIs; snapshot-cap abort via a lowered constant; unscoped reconciliation refused; `?limit=2` and `?limit=0` on the route. `pytest tests/test_document_*.py tests/test_preview_documents.py tests/test_preview_runtime.py tests/integration/test_document_application.py tests/integration/test_storage_reconciliation_postgres_live.py tests/integration/test_document_retention_postgres_live.py` = **194 passed, 2 skipped**.
- **Boundary:** the page size is a fixed server cap, not keyset pagination; clients cannot request more than 500 rows. Reconciliation tests that previously relied on an all-tenant sweep now reconcile each tenant explicitly.
- **Docs:** security register and SEC-010 section updated; SEC-010 removed from the open-findings gate.
## SEC-017 stable route error codes — 2026-11-13

- **Finding:** 125 `self._error(<status>, str(exc), ...)` sites in `http/project_lifecycle_routes.py` returned the exception message as the response `code`. Permission denials named the required grant (`authorization denied for permission=project.write`), and broad `except (TypeError, ValueError, KeyError)` handlers turned builtin faults into 400 responses carrying their reprs.
- **Fix (code `7d12efe6`):** a module helper `_stable_error_code(exc, fallback)` returns only declared opaque code segments (`CODE`, `CODE:anchor`, up to four colon-separated segments); permission-requirement text maps to `AUTHORIZATION_PERMISSION_DENIED` and `INVALID_AUTHORIZATION_<field>` maps to `INVALID_AUTHORIZATION_CONTEXT`. Prose, quotes and `KeyError` reprs are logged with `exc_info` and replaced by the site's fixed fallback. Every reflection site now uses the helper. Broad builtin handlers were split so `ValueError` keeps its documented 400 code while `TypeError`/`KeyError` return a fixed `500 INTERNAL_ERROR` (retryable) and reach 5xx alerting. The web calendar-holidays panel now matches `AUTHORIZATION_PERMISSION_DENIED`.
- **Tests:** `test_stable_error_code_never_returns_internal_detail` proves KeyError/TypeError prose, permission text and derived authorization-field text never pass through; `test_unexpected_route_fault_returns_a_fixed_internal_error_code` drives a fake activity API raising `KeyError("INTERNAL_SECRET")` through the real route and asserts `500 INTERNAL_ERROR` with no message text. Callers updated: lifecycle/activity/baseline/issue/preview route tests now assert the stable denial code. `pytest tests` = **3842 passed, 69 skipped**; `pytest tests/integration` = **254 passed, 107 skipped**; `apps/web` `npm test` = **1027 + 14 passed**.
- **Boundary:** application-layer exception classes still construct permission messages for logs and direct callers; only the HTTP boundary is sanitised. `scheduling/*` and `p6_*` calculators remain outside the reviewed surface.
- **Docs:** security register SEC-017 row and section updated, SEC-018 row corrected to its shipped fix `ebaec38a`, open-findings gate now closed.

## WS-004 window tabs, close, reorder and dirty state — 2026-11-13

- **Finding:** `workspaceHasPendingEdits` treated any non-empty field in the rendered activity/WBS create and edit forms as unsaved input. Those forms ship with defaults (`duration_value="1"`, WBS `sequence_number="0"`, the selected WBS code and name), so the Activities tab was permanently dirty: the dirty marker never cleared, closing the window always asked to discard changes, and leaving the workspace always prompted even with nothing typed.
- **Fix (code `0121d9d1`):** the predicate now counts a field only when `value` differs from its own rendered `defaultValue`, so untouched defaults stay clean while typed values (including drafts restored after an unrelated render) still count. Opening or dismissing the Project Properties overlay refreshes the marker from the overlay itself, and closing the window that owned the overlay removes it before re-syncing the remaining tabs.
- **Tests:** new `apps/web/e2e/workspace-window-tabs-v26.mjs` (contract `cubi-window-tabs-v26.v1`) drives the real preview through headless Chrome for 11 scenarios: default tabs, duplicate-open suppression, switching, third window plus non-active close, keyboard reorder with reload persistence, pointer drag reorder, dirty confirm/cancel and confirm/accept, last-window guard, restored set/order/active window, Persian RTL at 1280×720, and a no-write check. Evidence `docs/workspace/evidence/workspace-window-tabs-v26-2026-11-13/` records 11/11 passed at `0121d9d1`, zero exceptions/console errors/5xx, and `writes` limited to three `POST /api/projects/preview-rps-01/open` session handshakes. `apps/web` `npm test` = **1027 + 14 passed**.
- **Boundary:** tab management is presentation and browser-local (`cubi:workspace-windows`, `cubi:workspace-window-order`); it issues no schedule or project write and adds no permission. Help's "project-level windows close when all open projects close" has no project-close action in this single-project workspace, so that rule stays a documented gap.
- **Docs:** matrix WS-004 row moved from NOT_AUDITED to WIRED_WRITE and a §2.1 evidence row added (Help topic `using_main_windows.htm`).

## REL-001 relationship lifecycle and rejection feedback — 2026-11-13

- **Finding:** the Logic / Relationships panel was audited in the real browser for the first time. Creation, revision-guarded edit and delete already persisted through `p6-relationship-api.v1`, but a submit with the same activity twice returned silently (a lost click), and a delete button removed a scheduling constraint with no confirmation, unlike the Gantt arrow menu.
- **Fix (code `cdf27afb`):** invalid relationship input now renders a localized `[data-relationship-error]` notice in `#cp-logic`; only a genuine server rejection reaches `reportRelationshipError`. Deleting a row asks `relationshipDeleteConfirm` first and restores focus on cancel.
- **Tests:** new `apps/web/e2e/relationships-v26.mjs` (contract `cubi-relationships-v26.v1`) — 11/11 at `cdf27afb`: persisted list with pair/type/lag/revision, self-link notice, SS create with lag 2, reload persistence, edit to FF lag 3 with a higher record revision, Details Predecessors/Successors, delete cancel then accept, duplicate rejection, deleted row staying gone, viewer denial (`AUTHORIZATION_PERMISSION_DENIED`, no row), and only the two intentional rejection messages in the console. Evidence `docs/workspace/evidence/relationships-v26-2026-11-13/`. `apps/web` `npm test` = 1027 + 14 passed.
- **Boundary:** the Help's Details `Assign`/`Remove` path stays disabled with a reason; relationships are created from the Logic panel. No external/other-project relationship, comments, lag-calendar selector or predecessor/successor window.
- **Docs:** matrix REL-001 moved from NOT_AUDITED to WIRED_WRITE and a §2.1 row added (Help `add_predecessor_relationships.htm`, `linkingactivitieswithrelationships.htm`).

## WS-005 cross-window context menus for the record registers — 2026-11-13

- **Finding:** a fresh audit of every window's shortcut surface found only the Activities table/Gantt and the Activity Details pane bound to the viewport portal. Right-clicking a risk, threshold, expense, cost account or report row fell through to the browser menu, so the record windows had no way to reach the commands their own toolbars and detail panes already offered. The shared engine itself was already generic (fixed-position portal in `document.body`, edge flip and clamp, Escape with focus restore, outside-click dismissal, arrow roving, `dir=rtl`), and `workspaceContextAt` already returned null outside the Activities surfaces, so no ownership rule had to change.
- **Fix (code `24d694d0`):** new `apps/web/src/workspace-register-context-menu.ts` with `bindRegisterContextMenus` (delegated `contextmenu` plus `keydown` for `Shift+F10` and the Context Menu key) and the pure `buildRegisterMenuItems`. Every command names the selector of the control the window already renders: row-scope buttons for risks (`risk-edit`, `risk-delete`), thresholds (`threshold-edit`, `threshold-monitor`, `threshold-delete`), expenses (`expense-edit`, `expense-delete`) and cost accounts (`cost-account-edit`, `cost-account-copy`, `cost-account-default`, `cost-account-delete`); host-scope detail controls for reports (`report-edit`, `report-delete`, `report-run`). Right-click selects the row first, matching the desktop reference and rendering the report detail pane, and the menu is rebuilt from the live controls. `requiresWrite` commands stay visible but disabled with a localized reason for readers; a control that is absent or disabled for the record disables its command with the unavailable reason. `anchorCandidates` in `workspace-context-menu.ts` gained the five register row id attributes so Escape returns focus to the re-rendered record.
- **Tests:** new `apps/web/e2e/register-context-menus-v26.mjs` (contract `cubi-ws-005-register-context-menus.v1`) runs 65 checks against the live preview at 1440×900 and 1280×720: five register surfaces open at the pointer with the exact real command set and select their row; the portal stays in the viewport; Shift+F10, the Context Menu key, ArrowDown and Escape work and focus returns to the row; an outside click dismisses; Edit opens each real edit form and cancels back; Run Report produces stored rows; the Activities row menu is unchanged and a register toolbar right-click opens nothing; a viewer sees both write commands disabled with the permission reason while report Run stays enabled; Persian renders `dir=rtl` with Persian labels without overflowing; menu Delete removes the persisted risk (404). Evidence `docs/workspace/evidence/register-context-menus-v26-2026-11-13/` records 65/65 passed at `24d694d0`, zero exceptions/console errors/5xx and 11 screenshots. `apps/web/src/workspace-register-context-menu.test.ts` adds 6 cases. `apps/web` `npm test` = 1033 + 14 passed.
- **Boundary:** no request path is added; every command activates an existing button whose API contract and revision guard are unchanged (`p6-risk-api.v1`, `p6-threshold-api.v1`, `p6-cost-api.v1`, `p6-report-api.v1`). Permissions are unchanged and reflected, not bypassed. Help topic `use_shortcut_menus.htm`: “Instead of using standard menus and buttons, you can also use the right mouse button to access frequently used commands.”
- **Remaining gaps:** Resources, Assignments, Progress, Issues, Documents, Calendars, Roles, Administration, Tracking, Backup and Exchange still have no context menu; multi-selection menus, blank-area menus inside these registers and submenus are not implemented.
- **Docs:** matrix WS-005 row moved from NOT_AUDITED to WIRED_WRITE and a §2.1 evidence row added.

## PRJ-001 persisted project register and Business Domain hierarchy — 2026-11-13

- **Finding:** the Projects window rendered a register but had no persisted hierarchy at all. The CUBI information architecture replaces the P6 EPS level with **Business Domain** (Construction, Oil & Gas, Infrastructure, Industrial, Energy, Other), and no project record, API field or store column carried that classification, so any tree or group label would have been fabricated in the browser. Listing also returned every membership row without re-checking `PROJECT_READ`.
- **Fix (code `02369cbe`):** `business_domain` is a first-class field on the single existing project model in `project_lifecycle.py`, normalised through explicit aliases (`Oil & Gas`/`oil and gas`/hyphens/spaces) and rejected with `PROJECT_BUSINESS_DOMAIN_INVALID` otherwise. The preview store adds a defaulted column with an additive migration; the PostgreSQL repository and `infra/project_lifecycle_schema.sql` mirror it with a CHECK constraint. `PATCH` treats an absent field as `None` and both stores use `COALESCE`, so unrelated settings saves cannot clear a classification. `list_projects` now filters each membership through `PROJECT_READ`. The web register adds `ProjectRegisterState` and `renderProjectRegister` in `workspace-view.ts`: a Business Domain tree, search, domain filter, sort, row and keyboard selection, group collapse, a selection card that previews a record without opening it, and explicit empty/loading/no-match states; the create form and Project settings expose the classification in en and fa.
- **Tests:** `apps/web/e2e/project-register-v26.mjs` (contract `cubi-prj-001-project-register.v1`) — 38/38 checks at 1280×720 en, a Persian RTL cell and a Viewer cell, all expectations derived from `GET /api/projects`: exact rows/tenant/domain cells, tree groups and one `aria-current`, search/filter/no-match/reset, domain filter and start-date sort, selection preview then Open, Enter selection and collapse/expand, create→reload→PATCH to Energy with stale 409 and invalid 400, persistence after reload, and a Viewer read with a denied PATCH. Evidence `docs/workspace/evidence/project-register-v26-2026-11-13/` records 38/38 at `02369cbe`, zero exceptions/console errors/API failures and 10 screenshots. Python: `test_project_lifecycle.py`, `test_project_lifecycle_http_routes.py`, `test_preview_project_metadata_storage.py`, `test_postgres_project_lifecycle.py` cover normalisation, omission preservation, permission filtering and both stores; full `pytest tests` = 4107 passed / 176 skipped. `apps/web` `npm test` = 1036 + 14 passed.
- **Boundary:** the register reads and writes the existing `project-lifecycle` records only; it introduces no second project model, no client-side fixtures and no vendor terminology. Permissions are unchanged: `PROJECT_READ` for listing, `PROJECT_ADMIN` for create/update. Optimistic concurrency stays on `settings_revision`.
- **Remaining gaps:** Business Domain is one flat classification per project — no user-defined EPS nodes, parent/child domains, project codes or OBS; domains cannot be created or moved from the tree; the register has no column chooser, saved layouts, grouping by other fields or bulk actions; portfolio rollups are absent.
- **Docs:** matrix PRJ-001 moved from NOT_AUDITED to WIRED_WRITE and a §2.1 row added (Help `projects.htm`, `add_a_project_to_the_eps_hierarchy.htm`).

## PRJ-003 project tabs, switching and per-project windows — 2026-11-13

- **Finding:** the workspace held exactly one project context. Opening another project replaced it, reload restored only that one id, and nothing preserved the user's place when switching back. Two other defects surfaced while auditing the transition: each project shared one browser window set, and a project-settings overlay appended to `.cp-app-shell` survived a project switch, leaving an editor bound to the previous project's revision on screen.
- **Fix (code `915601d9`):** `apps/web/src/workspace-project-tabs.ts` stores the ordered open tab set and active id per user, filters it through the session's own project list, refuses to close the last tab and caps the readable set. `ProjectBootstrap` restores the set on start, refreshes `GET /api/projects` before `POST /api/projects/{id}/open`, activates a tab only after the server returns the context, opens the neighbour when the active tab closes, and activates only a project the session still lists. The desktop shell renders a project tab strip above the window tabs; the last tab has no close control. `main.ts` requires confirmation before a dirty switch or active close, treats a cancelled prompt as a no-op, and removes the previous project's settings overlay on transition. `workspace-windows.ts` adds `cubi:workspace-windows:<projectId>`, so each project tab restores its own desktop window set; a project opened for the first time starts from `DEFAULT_OPEN_WINDOWS`.
- **Tests:** `workspace-project-tabs.test.ts` (11), `project-bootstrap.test.ts` (7 new tab tests including the refreshed list), `workspace-desktop-shell.test.ts` (2), `workspace-windows.test.ts` (3). `apps/web/e2e/project-tabs-v26.mjs` (contract `cubi-prj-003-project-tabs.v1`) — 30/30 checks: single tab and absent close; second project opened from the register; per-project window sets; dirty cancel/confirm; inactive and active close with the re-authorized neighbour; reload restoration; external record save visible after switching plus stale 409; Persian RTL 1280×720; Viewer tab scope and a denied non-member open. Evidence `docs/workspace/evidence/project-tabs-v26-2026-11-13/` records status `passed` at `915601d9`, zero exceptions/console errors/API failures, 9 screenshots. `apps/web` `npm test` = 1059 + 14 passed.
- **Boundary:** tabs are browser-local presentation; every transition calls the existing open endpoint, which re-checks membership and `PROJECT_READ`. No scheduling, project-data or permission rule changed; metadata still uses `settings_revision` and a stale write returns 409.
- **Remaining gaps:** no exclusive/shared/read-only access mode, no multi-project aggregation, cross-project relationships or integrated WBS/OBS; no tab drag-reorder or keyboard cycling; no project-close command beyond closing the tab; unsaved edits are confirmed and discarded rather than saved.
- **Docs:** matrix PRJ-003 moved from NOT_AUDITED to WIRED_WRITE and a §2.1 row added (Help `open_a_project.htm`, `single_project_mode_vs_multi-project_mode.htm`).

## WBS-001 hierarchical WBS table and chart — 2026-11-13

- **Requirement:** the WBS window must present the persisted hierarchy as a table and as a chart, with the same nodes and the same order in both layouts. Official Help: `view_the_wbs.htm` (Project > WBS; View > Show on Top > Chart View / Table) and `change_the_work_breakdown_structure_table_display.htm`.
- **Implementation:** new `apps/web/src/workspace-wbs-hierarchy.ts` projects `WorkspaceWbsNode[]` into a single depth-first row list (sibling order = `sequenceNumber`, then local code, then ID; orphaned parents promoted and flagged; cycles/duplicate IDs visited once). `renderWbsTable` and `renderWbsChart` in `apps/web/src/workspace-view.ts` both read that list, so neither layout can invent or reorder rows. The table adds WBS Code, WBS Name, Activities (count of the loaded workspace activities), Sequence and Rev columns; both record elements remain focusable buttons carrying `data-wbs-id`.
- **Boundary:** reads are the existing `GET /api/v2/workspace/control-room/read` and `GET /api/projects/{id}/p6/wbs`; the view toggle is browser-local and writes nothing. Create/edit/delete endpoints were already wired and are unchanged in this slice.
- **Defect found and fixed:** the incremental selection update only refreshed `aria-current` on record buttons, so a table row kept the stale `aria-selected` value after a click. The WBS window now also refreshes `.cp-wbs-row[aria-selected]` on every incremental selection change.
- **Tests:** `workspace-wbs-hierarchy.test.ts` (7), a table↔chart order parity test in `workspace-view.test.ts`; 1067 `tsx` + 14 `node --test` pass, `tsc` and the esbuild bundle pass. Browser suite `apps/web/e2e/wbs-hierarchy-v26.mjs` (contract `cubi-wbs-001-table-chart.v1`) passes 19/19 at code `31c79ca6` with 7 screenshots, zero exceptions/console errors/5xx, including the RTL 1280×720 cell and a persisted depth-3 child after reload.
- **Remaining gaps:** no Gantt top layout for WBS, no column chooser, chart is an indented card map, and status/weighted milestones plus copy/cut/paste/delete reassignment are WBS-002 and WBS-003.

## WBS-002 WBS status, percent-complete technique and weighted milestones — 2026-11-13

- **Requirement:** the WBS Details surface must expose the persisted WBS status and the percent-complete technique, and weighted WBS milestones must roll up into a percent complete. Official Help: `wbs_status_types.htm` (Planned, Active, Inactive, What-If), `using_weighted_milestones_in_the_wbs.htm`, `assign_wbs_weighted_milestones.htm` and `display_work_breakdown_structure_details.htm`.
- **Implementation:** `src/construction_pm/wbs_master_repository.py` adds `WBSStatus` and `PercentCompleteTechnique` to `WBSMaster`, an additive SQLite/Postgres `status` and `percent_complete_technique` column migration, the `WBSMilestone` table (positive weight, completed flag, cascading delete) and `replace_milestones`, which validates unique IDs, scope and `expected_revision`, replaces the list and advances the WBS revision. `weighted_percent_complete` = completed weight ÷ total weight rounded to two decimals. `P6WBSMasterAPI` and `GET/PUT /api/projects/{id}/p6/wbs/{id}/milestones` expose the list behind `PROJECT_WRITE`; `PersistedWorkspaceReadProvider` projects status, technique, `percent_complete` (only for the milestones technique) and the milestone list. Web: `WorkspaceWbsNode` gains the same fields with contract validation; the WBS window General tab shows them and offers a status/technique form, and the WBS Milestones tab renders an editable weighted list with a live roll-up, add/remove rows and a revision-guarded save.
- **Boundary:** status, technique and milestones persist on the WBS master; the milestone list is replaced atomically and belongs to the node (delete cascades). `percent_complete` is derived only when the technique is `milestones`; activity-derived percent complete is untouched. The client roll-up mirror is display-only and never written.
- **Defect found and fixed:** a WBS mutation re-read replaced the whole workspace state and fell back to the default schedule window. `refreshWorkspaceAfterWbsMutation` now preserves the active window and locale, so a save does not close the window the user is working in.

## WBS-003 WBS copy, cut/paste and delete guidance — 2026-11-13

- **Requirement:** the WBS window must copy a WBS element and its descendants, cut and paste an element so its activity assignments follow it, and protect a delete against children and activity assignments. Official Help: `copy_and_paste_a_work_breakdown_structure_element.htm`, `cut_and_paste_a_work_breakdown_structure_element.htm` and `delete_a_work_breakdown_structure_element.htm`.
- **Implementation:** `copy_subtree` on both WBS stores duplicates a depth-first subtree beneath the selected target (root code from the request; descendants keep local code/name/sequence/status/technique; every record starts at revision 1 and milestone rows are copied; activity assignments are never copied). `P6WBSMasterAPI.copy` requires `PROJECT_WRITE`, a current source `expected_revision` and a same-scope target, and `POST /api/projects/{id}/p6/wbs/{wbs_id}/copy` returns the new `root_wbs_id` plus all copied node DTOs. Cut pastes through the existing `PATCH /p6/wbs/{id}` with a new parent, so the record identity and its `activity_wbs_assignment` rows are unchanged; a descendant target is rejected with `WBS_HIERARCHY_CYCLE`. Web: `workspace-wbs-clipboard.ts` holds the page-local clipboard and derives a sibling-unique copy code; the WBS window toolbar gains Cut/Copy/Paste with a clipboard label; the incremental WBS selection path now refreshes those commands' disabled state; a refused delete renders the localized V26 precondition in `[data-wbs-delete-guidance]` instead of `WBS_IN_USE`.
- **Boundary:** copy is a new server-side write returning new IDs; cut is the existing revision-guarded WBS update; delete is unchanged and still refuses nodes in use. The clipboard is browser-local and writes nothing. The pre-existing WBS write contract is preserved: stale revisions return 409 `REVISION_CONFLICT`, while invalid parents and sibling-code collisions keep their established 400 responses.
- **Defect found and fixed:** the incremental WBS selection update only refreshed rows and details, so the freshly added Cut/Copy/Paste commands kept their initial disabled state after a selection. The update path now also sets their disabled state from the selected element and the clipboard.
- **Tests:** `tests/test_wbs_master_repository.py` (6 copy cases), `tests/test_p6_activity_master_http_routes.py` (copy round trip, stale 409, sibling 400, missing source 404, missing target 400, viewer 403), `tests/integration/test_wbs_master_postgres_live.py` (live PostgreSQL copy), `apps/web/src/workspace-wbs-clipboard.test.ts` and a WBS toolbar interaction test. Browser suite `apps/web/e2e/wbs-cut-copy-paste-v26.mjs` (contract `cubi-wbs-003-cut-copy-paste.v1`) passes 24/24 at code `888512ca` with 7 screenshots, zero exceptions and zero 5xx. Full suites at the same code: Python 4123 passed / 178 skipped, Web 1080 + 14 passed.
- **Remaining gaps:** the Copy WBS Options / Copy Activity Options / Renumber Activity IDs dialogs are absent and activities are never copied; the V26 delete prompt's cascade-or-reassign choice is not offered (the delete is refused with guidance); Cut/Copy/Paste live on the WBS toolbar rather than the Edit menu or a context menu.
- **Docs:** matrix WBS-003 moved from NOT_AUDITED to WIRED_WRITE and a §2.1 row added (Help `copy_and_paste_a_work_breakdown_structure_element.htm`, `cut_and_paste_a_work_breakdown_structure_element.htm`, `delete_a_work_breakdown_structure_element.htm`).

## Production Vercel login and workspace continuation — 2026-11-13

- **Requirement:** open the deployed domain, sign in with a real account, see authorized projects, open one, see the full CUBI Workspace, and stay signed in after refresh — without Render, without preview personas and without a second shell.
- **Root cause found:** the `cubi-web-preview` deployment was a static-only bundle. Every `/api/*` path returned `404 NOT_FOUND`, there was no production login route, no session issuance, no DDL for the lifecycle tables, and the preview login was a fixture-persona page. `f60c09fa` could not log anyone in.
- **Implementation:** `src/construction_pm/identity_session.py` (scrypt password hashing, opaque `cp_session` tokens stored only as SHA-256, HMAC CSRF, absolute + idle expiry, per-email/IP throttling, pseudonymous IP hashing, generic authentication failures); `deployment/production_runtime.py` (PostgreSQL identity/lifecycle/workspace composition, bounded per-instance pool, request-scoped connection, readiness vs. liveness); `deployment/production_web.py` (login/logout, hardened cookies, Origin + CSRF enforcement, session-gated `/app`, non-traversable static delivery, HSTS); `deployment/schema.py` (idempotent table creation, never drops a row); `api/index.py` + `api/shell.py`; `vercel.json`, `scripts/vercel_build.sh`, `.vercelignore`, root `requirements.txt`; `apps/web/login.html`; `apps/web/src/csrf.ts` and CSRF-aware session/client requests; `scripts/provision_workspace.py` (init/user/project, password via stdin, curated registry + layout). Vercel build output (`public/`) is now ignored by Git.
- **Defects found and fixed during verification:** the Vercel build flattens bundles into `public/dist/`, while `StaticSite` only resolved `dist/web/src/`; the shell therefore loaded no JavaScript and the workspace never rendered. Resolution now accepts both layouts. Earlier fixes retained: session cookie must be named `cp_session` because the route layer reads that exact cookie; duplicate `Content-Length`/security headers are filtered after delegation; static traversal containment checks the real root; `sqlite`/`postgres` placeholder parity in the identity store.
- **Boundary:** identity and sessions persist in PostgreSQL (`identity_users`, `identity_sessions`, `identity_login_attempts`); project lifecycle in `project_lifecycle_*`; the workspace read composes the persisted activity/WBS/relationship/field-registry/layout repositories with no fabricated schedule facts. Preview personas and in-memory sessions are never reachable from the production composition.
- **Tests:** `tests/test_production_identity_session.py` 21 passed; `tests/test_production_web_app.py` 25 passed; `tests/integration/test_production_login_postgres_live.py` 10 passed against live PostgreSQL (real login, session, projects, open with CSRF, workspace v2 read, refresh, logout, post-logout 401, viewer 403 on write, planner write allowed/admin denied, admin project create, cross-tenant rejection, stale revision 409, 429 throttle); browser `apps/web/e2e/production-login-e2e.mjs` 10 checks with 3 screenshots (1280×720, 1366×768, Persian RTL), zero exceptions, console errors or API 5xx. Web suite 1087 + 14 passed; `npm run build` and `npm run typecheck` passed.
- **Deploy status:** `https://cubi-web-preview.vercel.app` and the immutable `…-qkm0lkylp-…` deployment still serve the old static bundle; `/api/health/live` remains 404. Publishing needs Vercel access (token/dashboard) or a Git-integrated redeploy of `main`, plus a production PostgreSQL DSN and the first user's password. No secret was written to Git.
- **Docs:** `docs/deploy/PRODUCTION_VERCEL.md`; matrix LIF-06 moved from `missing` to `wired-write` with the Vercel gateway recorded as `BLOCKED_EXTERNAL`, and OPS-02 updated with the observed 404 evidence.

## Production Vercel gateway reached — 2026-11-13 (follow-up to e8999339)

- **What changed:** the deployed project `cubi-web-preview` is rooted at `apps/web` (Vercel's own PR metadata reports `"rootDirectory":"apps/web"`). The repository-root `api/` functions and `vercel.json` were therefore invisible to it. Added the project-local layout: `apps/web/vercel.json`, `apps/web/api/index.py`, `apps/web/api/shell.py`, `apps/web/requirements.txt`, and `apps/web/scripts/vercel-project-build.mjs` + `prepare-vercel-runtime.mjs`. The build assembles the Pages artifact into `apps/web/vercel-site` (with `CUBI_WORKSPACE_URL` cleared so `/app` never redirects to Render), removes the static `app/` mirror, copies `login.html`, and stages an unmodified `src/` copy into `apps/web/vercel-runtime/src` for the functions. Both layouts (repository-root and `apps/web`) remain valid.
- **Configuration defects fixed:** `includeFiles` is a single glob string in the Vercel schema; the earlier array form invalidated the whole `vercel.json` and failed every deployment before the build. Per-function `memory`/`maxDuration` were removed to avoid plan-limit validation.

The Python function is live through the gateway; authentication is not faked while configuration is missing.
- **Staged-layout verification:** the `apps/web/vercel-runtime` entrypoints were driven over WSGI against PostgreSQL (`cubi_prod_test`): live/ready 200 (database healthy), anonymous `/app` 302, real login 200, `/api/session` 200, `/api/projects` 200 with the authorized project, `/app` 200 with the shell HTML.
- **Tests:** `tests/test_vercel_entrypoints.py` 6 passed (unconfigured liveness, readiness/login honesty, shell refusal, callable entrypoints, config shape); full Python suite 4176 passed / 188 skipped; `apps/web` typecheck and npm tests pass; the live-PostgreSQL integration module (10 tests) passes twice in a row against the same database (per-run client IP for the rate-limit case).
- **Remaining blocker (external, not code):** set `CONSTRUCTION_PM_DATABASE_URL`, `CONSTRUCTION_PM_SESSION_SECRET` (≥32) and `CONSTRUCTION_PM_PUBLIC_ORIGIN` on the Vercel project, provide a reachable PostgreSQL instance, and provision the first user with `scripts/provision_workspace.py` (password via stdin). LIF-06 stays `BLOCKED_EXTERNAL`; no real Vercel login has been observed. The other two Vercel projects on this repository fail with `There is no GitHub account connected to this Vercel account.` on every deployment, including main; that is pre-existing account state unrelated to this code.
- **Docs:** `docs/deploy/PRODUCTION_VERCEL.md` (project shape, build staging, observed gateway responses, exact inputs); matrix LIF-06 and OPS-02 updated.

## Production login/API security and runtime review — 2026-11-13

- **Scope:** the deployed login and API boundary at main `2be98f87` (production alias serves the build; the short domain is still pinned to the 16:23 static deployment). No demo identity was created, no project data was touched, and the official login URL was not changed.
- **Findings and fixes:**
  - Liveness and readiness opened a request transaction, so a database outage turned `/api/health/live` into a 500 and readiness raised instead of reporting. Both health paths now bypass the request transaction, readiness catches connection failures and reports `503 not_ready`, and application routes return `503 {"code":"SERVICE_UNAVAILABLE"}` without leaking details.
  - `CONSTRUCTION_PM_INSECURE_COOKIES` could disable the `Secure` cookie flag on an HTTPS origin. HTTPS now forces `Secure`; the escape hatch only applies to a loopback HTTP harness.
  - The HTTP boundary derived the throttle secret from the environment instead of the identity service's secret; `ProductionRuntime` now passes `session_secret` explicitly, and the client address prefers Vercel's single-value `x-real-ip` before `x-forwarded-for`.
  - `/login` was served by the CDN without security or cache headers. Both Vercel configurations now send `Cache-Control: no-store`, `nosniff`, `X-Frame-Options: DENY` and `Referrer-Policy: no-referrer` for `/login` and `/login.html`, and `no-store` for `/app/` and `/app.html`; the unconfigured function fallbacks send the same security headers.
- **Verified non-findings:** session tokens are opaque 256-bit values stored only as SHA-256; login always issues a new token; cookies are `HttpOnly`, `SameSite=Lax`, `Path=/`, `Secure`; CSRF uses a constant-time HMAC comparison and rejects missing/foreign origins; `/app` is gated by `IdentityService.resolve`; logout revokes the stored session and clears both cookies; redirects accept only same-origin absolute paths and reject `//`, backslashes and control characters; the production composition mounts no operational/preview/demo endpoints.
- **Tests:** full Python suite 4179 passed / 189 skipped; live PostgreSQL module 11 passed (new outage test); web suite passes; browser E2E 10/10 with screenshots. Status remains `BLOCKED_EXTERNAL` for login because the Vercel project still has no database DSN, session secret or public origin.

## Dual tool-rail customizable toolbar — 2026-11-14

- **Requirement:** replace the duplicated left/right rail icon maps and the contextual-command list with two independently customizable tool rails sharing one complete registry. The right rail must expose eighteen Version 26 families; every family needs Add or Remove; right-click must customize toolbars and icons; pointer and keyboard drag must move buttons between families/rails and dock whole toolbars; custom toolbars and all preferences must persist per user/project without touching project or layout data.
- **Implementation:** new `apps/web/src/workspace-toolbar-registry.ts` (one typed command table: stable id, localized CUBI label/tooltip, icon, families, windows, admin gate, disabled reason, handler target, default visibility, customization actions; six left and exactly eighteen right families). New `apps/web/src/workspace-toolbar-config.ts` (versioned `cubi:workspace-toolbars:v2:<user>` with `workspaces["project:<id>"]`, explicit set/ move/reset/custom-toolbar operations, legacy migration). New `apps/web/src/workspace-toolbar-view.ts` (both rails, per-family Add or Remove, body-level rail/icon/Add-or-Remove menus, Customize dialog, pointer drag with ghost/insertion line/pointer capture, Alt+Arrow reorder, RTL placement). `workspace-view.ts` and `main.ts` render and persist the session; `workspace-icons.ts` adds grip/chevron/lock/move/style glyphs; both stylesheet copies receive the same 632-line toolbar block.
- **Boundary:** toolbar preferences live only in the versioned localStorage document; no project, Activity Layout, schedule or cost record is written. Disabled commands carry a reason and no executable target; existing APIs still enforce `PROJECT_WRITE`/administrator server-side.
- **Defects found and fixed:** cross-family moves duplicated a command because normalization re-added the missing registry entry (source now keeps a hidden entry); custom toolbars could not add commands (absent commands are now appended for custom families); the add-remove menu opened behind the Customize dialog (dialog closes first); the custom toolbar Delete action was missing from the rail context menu (added beside Rename); a synthetic document-level Escape exposed `target?.closest` assuming an Element (guarded with a `closest` function check).
- **Tests:** `apps/web/src/workspace-toolbar-rails.test.ts` (11 registry/config/render tests), adapted `workspace-view.test.ts`; 1117 `tsx` + 14 `node --test` pass; `tsc --noEmit` and `npm run build` pass. Browser `apps/web/e2e/toolbar-rails-v26.mjs` passes 24/24 checks with 17 screenshots and zero exceptions/console errors/5xx at code `7aa9a2d4d29e5492c212ea8bfaf8c74eed13d9a2`.

Acceptance matrix: `docs/qa/V26_DUAL_RAIL_TOOLBAR_MATRIX_2026-11-14.md`.
- **Remaining limits:** the "floating" state is a horizontal dock above the workspace without free saved coordinates; icon style is family-level; browser coverage is en/fa while other locale packs use fallback; preferences are browser-local. Enterprise/administrator/publish/report commands remain disabled with reasons. P4 formula lifecycle remains `BLOCKED_EXTERNAL`; PR #1923 stays open and is not itself completion evidence.
- **CI alignment:** `82d1cc1d` updates the pre-existing `apps/web/e2e/preview-e2e.mjs` journey for the rail-owned assistant (`action.assistant` toggling `#cp-ai-assistant-chat`, no public floating launcher on `/app`), `[data-nav-flyout]` navigation, the right-rail scroll container and the failed-bootstrap page. The full preview suite passes locally with 97 checks and 51 screenshots; `web`, PostgreSQL, Python 3.11/3.12/3.13 and all client typechecks pass on `82d1cc1d`. Toolbar implementation code remains `7aa9a2d4`; this commit is test/doc-only.

## PRJ-002 stored project fields — 2026-11-14

- **Requirement:** Project Details must expose OBS responsible manager, leveling priority, checkout and web-site as real stored project state, not display-only controls, with permissions, revision/lost-update protection, validation, reload readback and en/fa.

Create/PATCH normalize the manager (160 max) and require an absolute http/https web-site URL with a host, no credentials and 2048 max. `POST /api/projects/{id}/checkout` (`project-checkout-api.v1`, `PROJECT_ADMIN`) implements an exclusive lease with conditional SQL in both stores; a repeated state is an idempotent no-op, check-in is owner-only, and another user's metadata or status save returns 409. Leveling priority 1–100 writes through the existing canonical `p6-project-schedule-settings-api.v1` record revision rather than a duplicate column.
