## 2026-11-26 — P0 Engineering Phase E (Activities window slice 17): layout undo/redo, redo of a new edit and revision restore — Issue #2138

- **main SHA at branch start:** `2798ba9cec7e11e47054d385e736beb180beb777` (verified `origin/main` after the slice 16 merge of PR #2172).
- **Branch / head:** `feat/engineering-layout-undo-redo`, feature/test commit `c5c92c4302235717e865931ffe30cb583b88ce01`. PR **#2173**.
- **Audit result:** the revision-based layout history already existed — `layoutHistoryStore`, the Settings history bar (`↶ Undo` / `↷ Redo` / revision select / restore), Ctrl+Z/Ctrl+Y and menu dispatch, server `/revisions` fallback on a first undo, optimistic `baseRevision` on every save and `REVISION_CONFLICT` → reload + notice. What was missing was browser proof, and the pass exposed a real defect: `hydrateGroupSortFromLayout` read `metadata.groupSort` but keyed its memoisation *after* the read, so undoing to a revision that had no saved grouping plan left the previous three-band Group and Sort plan rendered under the new revision number (probe: 3 status bands at R3, baseline R1 band count 0).
- **Change:** no engine rewrite and no new store. `hydrateGroupSortFromLayout` now keys on `tenant:project:scope:revision` **before** reading the metadata, and a revision without a plan falls back to the canonical `wbs asc` group + `wbs asc` sort. Added `apps/web/e2e/layout-undo-redo-v26.mjs` (contract `cubi-layout-undo-redo-v26.v1`, 9 scenarios) covering the empty-history notice, column-width undo/redo, a new edit clearing the redo stack, Ctrl+Z/Ctrl+Y, reload followed by a server-backed undo, restore of the oldest revision (plus recoverability of the replaced revision) and the Group and Sort plan undo/redo.
- **Help authority:** P6 Professional v26 HTML Help — **Using Undo** (`using_undo.htm`), which scopes Undo to data edits on the General/Status/Resources/Relationships tabs and states that layout changes do not clear the Undo history, and **Layout toolbar** (`layout_toolbar.htm`), which lists New/Open/Save/Save As/Bars/Columns/Timescale. There is **no** layout undo/redo or revision-restore topic, so the CUBI layout revision history is recorded as a documented extension rather than a parity claim.
- **Verified behaviour:** undo restores the previous column width and header inline style, redo reapplies it, a new edit clears redo, keyboard and history-bar paths agree, a reload disables redo while undo still walks the server revisions, restoring R1 returns the width and the replaced revision stays reachable, and undoing a saved three-level status grouping returns to 0 field bands / 7 WBS rows with redo restoring the 3 bands. Every undo/redo/restore appends a revision; no conflict screen appeared because each step used the true head.
- **Tests (measured on this branch):** web `npm test` — **1,371 / 1,371** and preview-server **16 / 16**; `tsc --noEmit` exit 0; `npm run build` exit 0; full Python suite (DSN unset) — **5,311 passed / 272 skipped / 15 subtests / 0 failed**; `node --check` on the E2E passes.
- **Browser evidence (local full-stack preview, fresh SQLite file per run, real WSGI, real Chrome):** `apps/web/e2e/layout-undo-redo-v26.mjs`, **9/9 scenarios PASS**, 12 screenshots, 0 exceptions/console errors/HTTP 5xx, en/LTR at 1440×900 and 1920×1080 and fa/RTL at 1280×720. The report records `testedCodeSha`/`gitHead` `c5c92c430`. Evidence: `docs/workspace/evidence/layout-undo-redo-v26-2026-11-26/`.
- **Open PR ownership check:** #2048, #1716 and #1698 remain `CONFLICTING`/`DIRTY` and touch `workspace-view.ts` / `styles.css` / `public/styles.css`. This slice touches `apps/web/src/main.ts`, adds one E2E file and updates four docs; it does not edit `workspace-view.ts`, `workspace-dialogs.ts`, `styles.css` or any shared layout module.
- **Docs:** compliance matrix rows for layout save/open were split — undo/redo/restore is a separate row citing `using_undo.htm`, the Help only covering data edits, the defect and fix, and a dated acceptance section; `ENGINEERING_WORKSPACE_ACTIVITIES.md` adds two `ENG-WS-LAYOUTS` rows, scenario `ENG-WS-ACT-12`, a divergence row and 19/19 Help-topic and 16/16 User Guide URL verification; `WEB_SURFACE_TRACEABILITY_MATRIX.md` expands the settings row and adds resolved **GAP-V26-05**.
- **Not changed:** no CPM/calendar/working-time calculation, no snapshot or day-facts contract, no data-edit undo, no parallel preference store, no API or storage change, no Production or Supabase write, no Oracle/Primavera/P6 branding.
- **Honest limits:** the layout history is a CUBI extension because V26 Help has no layout undo topic; the redo stack is per-session memory (a reload leaves only server-backed undo) and no data-edit undo exists in the Activities surface. The official Preview alias returned `DEPLOYMENT_NOT_FOUND`, so all browser evidence is local full-stack. Preview ≠ Production.
- **Status:** docs+evidence commit followed by CI; merge only after every required CI job is green.

## 2026-11-27 — P0 Engineering Phase E (Activities window slice 19): line numbers, Table Font and Row and User Preferences — Issue #2138

- **main SHA at branch start:** `aed07405d789f2f8daedda9ee39317666f35539f` (verified `origin/main` after the slice 18 merge of PR #2174).
- **Branch / head:** `feat/engineering-layout-line-numbers-prefs`.
- **Audit result:** line numbers, the Table Font and Row dialog and the User Preferences dialog were implemented but had never been exercised in a browser (the compliance matrix still said "implemented, not browser-tested"). The first browser pass verified the `#` column and exposed two real defects: `readLayoutPatch` applied the numeric height regardless of the chosen row-height mode, so "Keep Current" changed the heights it promises to keep and "Optimize by content" moved the minimum height; and the `Reset band format` button in User Preferences submitted the shared table fieldset, silently committing unsaved table edits.
- **Change:** `readLayoutPatch` reads the height only in Fixed height mode (mode read first, legacy payloads without a mode still apply); `syncRowHeightInput` locks and live-unlocks the height field from the chosen radio (one delegated `change` branch in `workspace-desktop-shell.ts`); a `wbsReset` submit skips the table block so the band reset stays scoped. No engine, store or CSS change.

## 2026-11-27 — Engineering Activity Operations: review fixes, V26 parity and verification — Issue #2138

**Branch / base:** `feat/eng-activity-operations`, synced with `main` `aed07405` through merge commit `ef7147eb` (no force-push, no history rewrite), then the Stage 3 copy fix `1196881d` (PR #2175 kept open). No merge, no Production/Supabase write, no second implementation of the operations.

- **Scope:** resolve the four Codex review findings on the authoritative activity Cut/Copy/Paste/Delete path, document the P6 V26 behavioral comparison, verify the security/transactional boundary, and run every suite against this exact code. Browser verification on the canonical Preview remains blocked (below).
- **Stage 0/1 (safe sync with main):** fetched `main` (`aed07405`) and the PR branch; main had advanced past the merge base with the Activity Details window-scoping and V26 presentation work. Merged `origin/main` into the branch with a real merge commit `ef7147eb` (no rebase, no force-push, no history rewrite). Two documentation files conflicted and both sides were preserved: `WEB_SURFACE_TRACEABILITY_MATRIX.md` now carries main's three-level Group and Sort, calendar-non-working toggle, undo/redo and Activity Details rows together with this branch's operations endpoint and `GAP-ENG-OPS-01`; `PROJECT_STATE_LEDGER.md` keeps all 2026-11-26 entries followed by this 2026-11-27 entry. `apps/web/src/main.ts` auto-merged and still composes both the details-preference work and `ActivityOperationJournal`/`runActivityOperation`. Migration step 23 is still the last registry step and main did not touch `deployments/migrations.py` since the merge base, so migration/RLS ordering is unchanged.
- **Four findings and resolutions (Stage A):**
  1. **A1 — copied activity kept the source Remaining Duration.** `_copy` no longer writes `remaining_duration`; a copied activity is canonically not-started and the scheduler derives remaining duration. Assignment `remaining_units`/`remaining_cost` reset to planned units/cost. Covered end to end by the composer publish/materialize test.
  2. **A2 — idempotency across an uncertain Paste retry.** `activity-operation-idempotency.ts` keeps one key **and the armed expected revisions** per pending operation; a lost response replays the committed result instead of duplicating. A new clipboard selection cancels the journal.
  3. **A3 — delete confirmation was coerced with `bool()`.** `confirm_dependencies` must now be a strict JSON Boolean at both the HTTP boundary and the service; any other value (`"false"`, 0, 1, null) returns **400 `P6_ACTIVITY_CONFIRM_DEPENDENCIES_INVALID`** and the database is unchanged.
  4. **A4 — relationship cascade double-counted.** `dependency_counts["relationships"]` uses `count(DISTINCT relationship_id)` across both endpoints, and removed-row totals accumulate instead of overwriting, so a relationship whose predecessor and successor are both deleted counts once.
- **Stage B (P6 V26 parity):** added a dated section to `docs/qa/ACTIVITIES_V26_COMPLIANCE_MATRIX_2026-11-08.md` covering the seven Help topics (add activity p.199; copy/paste p.208; Copy Activity Options p.560; Renumber p.210; cut/paste p.209; delete/dissolve p.206; durations p.202; activity dates p.196–198) with matches, intentional CUBI safety enhancements, documented differences and the exact next implementation. `docs/engineering/WEB_SURFACE_TRACEABILITY_MATRIX.md` gains `GAP-ENG-OPS-01` and the schedule-surface row now lists the operations endpoint.
- **Stage 3 (Resource Dependent copy review) — committed as `1196881d`:** reviewed the ten RD copy scenarios against the composer rules. A not-started RD source keeps its planned demand and copies cleanly. A progressed source whose stored period rows already describe the full planned contract (for example Actual 0 with Remaining equal to Planned) also copies. A progressed source whose rows describe only remaining demand cannot be reconciled with the reset planned units, and CUBI has no calendar-aware planned-demand spreader (`resources/loading.spread_units` is calendar-day based and unused, and a new one here would be a parallel engine). The copy therefore calls the same `validate_resource_dependent_demand_distribution` the composer uses and, on mismatch, raises `P6_ACTIVITY_COPY_PROGRESS_DEMAND_UNSUPPORTED:<activity>:<assignment>:<reason>` inside the transaction, so no activity, assignment or period row is written and the source is untouched. This is recorded as the open **GAP-ENG-OPS-02** with the exact next action (a real calendar-aware demand re-planning step in `_copy`), not as parity. The pre-fix code produced the inconsistent copy, so all three refusal tests fail on it.
- **Stage C (security/transactional evidence):** viewer **403** (unit, HTTP, preview, live PostgreSQL) with the DB unchanged; planner **allowed**; **project_admin** allowed only through the server-issued session role; a request body that claims `roles`/`is_admin`/`user_id` cannot elevate a viewer; stale revisions **409**; missing `Idempotency-Key` **400**; duplicate requests replay with no duplicate row; cut/paste changes only WBS and preserves relationships/progress; internal relationships copied and external ones reported; two-stage delete with strict Boolean confirmation; append-only audit; migration step 23 creates and hardens `p6_activity_operation` + `p6_activity_operation_audit` with restrictive RLS; exactly one operations endpoint (unknown subpaths and non-POST methods are rejected).
- **Stage E (tests on this exact code `1196881d`, DSN `postgresql://…/cubi_test`):**
  - Python full suite with the live DSN: **5,657 passed / 20 skipped / 15 subtests**, 0 failed.
  - Focused Python (operations unit + HTTP + composer + live PostgreSQL module): **62 passed**.
  - Live PostgreSQL operations module: **6 passed** (migration 23; copy commits relationships/assignments; viewer denied without writing; delete cascade + audit; both-endpoint relationship counted once; progressed RD copy refused without writing).
  - Web: **1,388 unit + 16 preview-server passed**, 0 failed; `tsc --noEmit` clean; production `npm run build` succeeded.
- **Stage D (canonical Preview):** designated URL `https://cubi-web-preview-git-feat-vercel-p-8eb184-foroughichamshir-7811.vercel.app/app?project=preview-rps-01&view=live` still returns **HTTP 404 `DEPLOYMENT_NOT_FOUND`** (anonymous curl, 2026-11-27). The hostname is Vercel's per-Git-branch preview alias and its branch slug is `feat-vercel-p…` (closest remote branch: `feat/vercel-preview-fixtures`, head `9e8310a1`), not `feat/eng-activity-operations`; the alias's deployment has been removed, so a build of this PR is not what that hostname serves. The alias also belongs to Vercel team `foroughichamshir-7811`, whose account is not connected to GitHub; no Vercel token or login exists in this sandbox, and the working `cubi-web-preview` deployment lives under a different team. No substitute URL was used. Browser E2E for the four commands is therefore **not run on the canonical Preview** and is not claimed.
- **Honest limits:** no per-checkbox Copy Activity Options UI; expenses, activity codes, WP-docs, notebook and financial-period data are not copied; dissolve is not implemented; a progressed Resource Dependent copy whose stored rows do not already reconcile with the planned contract is refused fail-closed with `P6_ACTIVITY_COPY_PROGRESS_DEMAND_UNSUPPORTED` (the operation deliberately does not invent demand/dates) and stays open as **GAP-ENG-OPS-02**, whose exact next action is a calendar-aware demand re-planning step in `_copy`; the clipboard does not persist across sessions. Preview ≠ Production; numeric parity against a licensed V26 run remains unproven.
- **Status:** four findings fixed with regression tests; code/DB/Web verified on this commit. PR #2175 stays open and is **not** merged; the stage is **not** declared complete while browser verification on the canonical Preview is externally blocked.

## 2026-11-28 — Engineering Activity Operations: relationship lag-calendar cascade/copy fixes, main sync and verification — Issue #2184 (PR #2175)

**Branch / base:** `feat/eng-activity-operations`, synced with `main` `9eb0e8e8` through merge commit `296e5c9b` (real merge commit; no rebase, no force-push, no history rewrite). PR #2175 stays open; no merge, no Production/Supabase write. The two new Codex threads are `PRRT_kwDOUoVPb868opC6` (P1) and `PRRT_kwDOUoVPb868opC9` (P2); they are distinct from the four previously resolved threads.

- **Step 1 (safe sync with main):** re-read `main` (`9eb0e8e8`) and the branch; `main` had advanced nine commits past the previous head (`9659ec68`) and the PR was `CONFLICTING`. Merged `origin/main` with a real merge commit `296e5c9b`. Three documentation files conflicted and both sides were preserved: `WEB_SURFACE_TRACEABILITY_MATRIX.md` (main's WBS/schedule/undo/details rows + this branch's operations endpoint and `GAP-ENG-OPS-01/02`), `ACTIVITIES_V26_COMPLIANCE_MATRIX_2026-11-08.md` (main's newer Network/Usage/Trace-Logic/screens rows + this branch's real-operations section) and `PROJECT_STATE_LEDGER.md` (all 2026-11-27 entries kept, then this entry). `main` did not touch `deployments/migrations.py` since the merge base, so migration step 23 ordering is unchanged.
- **Step 2 (P1, thread `PRRT_kwDOUoVPb868opC6`) — remove lag-calendar overrides with deleted relationships:** deleting an activity cascaded `relationship_master` but left the `relationship_lag_calendar_assignment` row, so the next schedule composition failed with `RELATIONSHIP_LAG_ASSIGNMENT_UNKNOWN_RELATIONSHIP`. `delete_dependents` now collects every affected `relationship_id` once (`relationship_ids_for_activities`, `DISTINCT` across both endpoints) and deletes their lag rows in the **same transaction, before** the relationship rows. The overrides are counted in `dependency_counts["lag_calendar_assignments"]`, included in the cascade confirmation set and the audit `removed_rows`; a relationship whose predecessor and successor are both selected is counted/deleted exactly once. Any later-step failure rolls the whole transaction back (lag rows and relationships survive).
- **Step 3 (P2, thread `PRRT_kwDOUoVPb868opC9`) — preserve relationship-specific lag calendars when copying:** `_copy` now records an old→new relationship-id mapping for copied internal relationships and `copy_relationship_lag_assignments` re-creates each override for the new relationship with the **same** `option`/`calendar_id`/`calendar_version`, `record_revision=1` and the new revision/project scope (validated with `RelationshipLagCalendarAssignmentMaster`). Only internal relationships are mapped, so an external relationship is never re-bound; a source without an override yields no row (never invented); the source row is unchanged. Result key: `lag_calendar_assignments_created`.
- **Step 4 (P6 V26 Help distinction):** read «Choose a calendar for relationship lag» (p.296; `Tools > Schedule > Options > General > Calendar for Scheduling Relationship Lag`) — a **project-wide** schedule option, not a per-relationship override. CUBI's per-relationship `relationship_lag_calendar_assignment` is a documented CUBI extension; the fix only preserves data and keeps the existing calculation contract, adding no parallel engine and changing no calculation behavior.
- **Pre-fix proof:** on the pre-fix worktree the new delete tests failed (`lag rows after delete: ['R-1']` with the relationship already gone) and the copy left `copied lag: None`; the new unit/HTTP/live/composer tests fail before the fix.
- **Step 5 (verification on this exact code, DSN `postgresql://cubi:cubi@127.0.0.1:5432/cubi_test`):** focused operations + HTTP **35 passed**; authoritative schedule composer **33 passed** (incl. the three lag tests); live PostgreSQL operations module **8 passed** (two new lag tests); full Python suite **5,671 passed / 20 skipped / 15 subtests / 0 failed**; Web `npm test` **1,391 unit + 16 preview-server, 0 failed**; `tsc --noEmit` clean; production `npm run build` succeeded. Migration: step 23 still the last registry step, `main` added none; `p6_activity_operation` and `p6_activity_operation_audit` both report `relrowsecurity = t` in the live database. Two explicit rollback tests (delete and copy) prove project data and lag rows are unchanged when a later step in the transaction fails.
- **Step 6 (Preview):** the main Preview `https://cubi-web-preview-git-main-foroughichamshir-7811.vercel.app/login?next=/workbench` is live (**HTTP 200**, sign-in page). The branch-specific alias for `feat/eng-activity-operations` does **not** resolve (`HTTP 000`, same-day probe), and the team account is not connected to GitHub, so a build of this branch is not deployed. Branch browser E2E for the copy/delete lag scenarios is therefore **BLOCKED** and is not claimed; the main Preview is not a substitute.
- **Honest limits:** no per-checkbox Copy Activity Options UI; expenses/codes/WP-docs/notebook/financial-period not copied; dissolve absent; progressed RD copy that cannot reconcile stays fail-closed (`GAP-ENG-OPS-02` open, not closed by a workaround or false parity). Preview ≠ Production; numeric parity against a licensed V26 run remains unproven.
- **Status:** both new defects fixed with unit/HTTP/live-PostgreSQL/composer proof; docs updated; commit + push next; PR #2175 remains open and **not merged** pending owner approval.
